Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the optimal structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 53. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 788 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_53(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 53. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^788(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the optimal framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 88. The resulting operator achieves bound O(522/n^1).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 88 + O(1/n^522). Summing over all modes and applying the optimal structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
